        details: asset.details,\n              });\n            }\n          }\n\n          // Update financial profile\n          if (analysis.extractedData.income?.monthlyAmount) {\n app.get(\"/api/cashflow/analysis\", isAuthenticated, async (req: any, res) => {\n    try {\n      const userId = req.user.claims.sub;\n      \n      // Get user's financial data\n      const debts = data.income.monthlyAmount);\n          }\n          \n          // Extract expenses from bank statements\n          if (doc.documentType === \"bank-statements\" && data.expenses) {\n            essentialExpenses += data.expenses.essential || 0;\n            discretionarySpending += data.expenses.discretionary || 0;\n          }\n          \n          // Extract savings\n          if (data.accountBalance) {\n            currentSavings += data.accountBalance;\n          }\n        }\n      });\n\n      // If no detailed expense data, estimate based on income\n      if discretionary\n      }\n\n      // Calculate safe monthly EXTRA\n      const netCashflow = monthlyIncome - essentialExpenses - minimumDebtPayments - discretionarySpending;\n      \n      // Safety += 20;\n      if (documents.length > 3) confidenceScore += 15;\n      if (currentSavings > monthlyIncome) confidenceScore += 15;\n      confidenceScore = Math.min(100, confidenceScore);\n\n      // currentSavings,\n        safeMonthlyExtra,\n        confidenceScore,\n        recommendations,\n      };\n\n      res.json(analysis);\n    } catch (error: any) {\n      console.error(\"Error All Rights Reserved.\n * Security monitoring and suspicious activity detection\n */\n\nimport { Request } from \"express\";\nimport { log } from \"./vite\";\n\ninterface SuspiciousActivity {\n  timestamp: Date;\n  ip: string;\n  userId?: string;\n  type: string;\n  details: string;\n  userAgent?: string;\n}\n\n// In-memory tracking (in production, use Redis or database)\nconst recentRequests = new Map<string, number[]>();\nconst suspiciousActivities: SuspiciousActivity[] = [];\n\n// Configuration\nconst RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW = 60000; // 1 minute\nconst MAX_REQUESTS_PER_WINDOW = 500; // Max requests per IP per minute (increased for testing)\nconst MAX_AUTH_ATTEMPTS = 5; // Max login attempts\nconst SUSPICIOUS_PATTERNS = [\n  /(\\.\\.|\\/\\/|\\\\\\\\)/,  // Path traversal attempts\n  /<script|javascript:|onerror|onclick/i,  // XSS attempts\n  /union.*select|drop.*table|insert.*into|delete.*from/i,  // SQL injection\n  /\\$\\{|%24%7B|%2524%257B/,  // Template injection\n  /\\.\\.\\//,  // Directory traversal\n];\n\n// Track request rates (non-blocking, minimal overhead)\nexport function trackRequest(req: Request): boolean {\n  const ip = req.ip || req.socket.remoteAddress || 'unknown';\n  const now = Date.now();\n  \n  // Get or create request history for this IP\n  if (!recentRequests.has(ip)) {\n    recentRequests.set(ip, []);\n  }\n  \n  const requests = recentRequests.get(ip)!;\n  \n  // Remove old requests outside the window\n  const validRequests = requests.filter(time => now - time < RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW);\n  \n  // Add current request\n  validRequests.push(now);\n  recentRequests.set(ip, validRequests);\n  \n  // Check if rate limit exceeded\n  if (validRequests.length > MAX_REQUESTS_PER_WINDOW) {\n    logSuspiciousActivity({\n      timestamp: new Date(),\n      ip,\n      type: 'RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED',\n      details: `${validRequests.length} requests in ${RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW}ms window`,\n      userAgent: req.headers['user-agent'] as string\n    });\n    return false; // Block request\n  }\n  \n  return true; // Allow request\n}\n\n// Check for suspicious patterns in request\nexport function checkSuspiciousPatterns(req: Request): boolean {\n  const ip = req.ip || req.socket.remoteAddress || 'unknown';\n  const checkString = `${req.path}${JSON.stringify(req.query)}${JSON.stringify(req.body)}`;\n  \n  for (const pattern of SUSPICIOUS_PATTERNS) {\n    if (pattern.test(checkString)) {\n      logSuspiciousActivity({\n        timestamp: new Date(),\n        ip,\n        type: 'SUSPICIOUS_PATTERN',\n        details: `Pattern detected: ${pattern.toString()} in request`,\n        userAgent: req.headers['user-agent'] as string\n      });\n      return false; // Block request\n    }\n  }\n  \n  return true; // Allow request\n}\n\n// Log suspicious activity (async, non-blocking)\nexport function logSuspiciousActivity(activity: SuspiciousActivity): void {\n  // Add to in-memory log\n  suspiciousActivities.push(activity);\n  \n  // Keep only last 1000 activities in memory\n  if (suspiciousActivities.length > 1000) {\n    suspiciousActivities.shift();\n  }\n  \n  // Log to console (in production, send to monitoring service)\n  log(`⚠️ SECURITY: ${activity.type} from ${activity.ip} - ${activity.details}`);\n}\n\n// Track failed authentication attempts\nconst authAttempts = new Map<string, number>();\n\nexport function trackAuthAttempt(identifier: string, success: boolean): boolean {\n  if (success) {\n    // Reset on successful auth\n    authAttempts.delete(identifier);\n    return true;\n  }\n  \n  // Track failed attempt\n  const attempts = (authAttempts.get(identifier) || 0) + 1;\n  authAttempts.set(identifier, attempts);\n  \n  if (attempts >= MAX_AUTH_ATTEMPTS) {\n    logSuspiciousActivity({\n      timestamp: new Date(),\n      ip: identifier,\n      type: 'EXCESSIVE_AUTH_ATTEMPTS',\n      details: `${attempts} failed authentication attempts`\n    });\n    return false; // Block further attempts\n  }\n  \n  return true; // Allow authAttempts.delete(ip);\n}\n\n// Cleanup old data periodically (runs every 5 minutes)\nsetInterval(() => {\n  const now = Date.now();\n  \n  // Clean up old request tracking data\n  for (const [ip, \n    try {\n      // Process files in batches\n      for (const file of files.filter(f => f.status === \"pending\")) {\n        setFiles((prev) =>\n          prev.map((f) =>\n            f.id === featuring the \"Timber\" AI assistant with \"Autopilot Money Ops\" philosophy. Built with React, Express, PostgreSQL, and OpenAI (GPT-4o), the application transforms messy financial documents into automated money management. The platform offers mobile-first document upload, AI-powered analysis, and tiered pricing plans. Timber Money empowers users to move from financial chaos to true autopilot management.\n\n## User Preferences\n\n### Design & UX\n- **Design System**: Stripe-inspired professional aesthetic\n- **Color Scheme**: Dark mode by default with light mode support\n- **Security Messaging**: Emphasize \"bank-level encryption\" throughout UI\n- **Trust Signals**: Professional financial statements, clear privacy policy\n\n### Privacy & Security\n- **Data Handling**: Documents processed in real-time, not permanently stored\n- **Encryption**: Bank-level AES-256 encryption\n- **Compliance**: Privacy policy and terms of service included\n- **User Control**: Easy data export and account deletion\n\n### Monetization\n- Free tier to attract users\n- Pro tier ($19/month) for power users\n- Family tier ($39/month) for households\n- 14-day refund policy\n- Stripe integration for secure payments\n\n## System Architecture\n\n### Tech Stack\n- **Frontend**: React 18, Vite, Tailwind CSS, shadcn/ui components\n- **Backend**: Express.js with TypeScript\n- **Database**: PostgreSQL with Drizzle ORM\n- **Authentication**: Replit Auth (OIDC-based)\n- **AI**: OpenAI GPT-4 for document analysis and financial advice\n- **Payments**: Stripe for subscriptions\n- **Session Management**: PostgreSQL-backed sessions\n\n### Key Features\n1. **Document Upload & Analysis**: Dual upload system with AI-powered data extraction and bank-level encryption\n   - **AI Vision Analysis** (POST /api/documents/upload): OpenAI GPT-4 vision analyzes images and extracts financial data automatically\n   - **PDF/OCR Extraction** (POST /api/docs/upload): Direct text extraction from PDFs using pdf-parse and OCR for images using Tesseract.js\n   - Supports 6 document types: bank statements, credit cards, loans, credit reports, investments, income documents\n   - Mobile-first upload with camera capture and photo library support\n   - Documents processed in real-time, not permanently stored\n   - Enhanced error handling ensures UI resilience during upload failures\n2. **Financial Profile Dashboard**: Provides complete asset/liability tracking, budget visualizations, income/expense analysis, and credit score tracking.\n3. **Debt Payoff Strategies**: Offers Avalanche, Snowball, and hybrid methods with side-by-side comparisons.\n4. **AI Financial Advisor**: A chat-based Q&A interface providing personalized, context-aware advice based on user financial profiles and uploaded documents.\n5. **Subscription Plans**: Includes Free, Pro, and Family tiers with varying features and access levels.\n\n### Database Schema\n- **Users**: Managed by Replit Auth with subscription tracking (stripeCustomerId, stripeSubscriptionId, subscriptionStatus, subscriptionPlan); session management via PostgreSQL.\n- **Documents**: Enhanced schema for dual upload system\n  - Core fields: userId, fileName, fileType, documentType, status, analysisData (stores both AI analysis and PDF/OCR extracted data)\n  - PDF/OCR fields: size, pages, sourcePath, needsReview\n  - Supports both AI vision analysis and direct text extraction workflows\n- **Debts**: Tracks debts with principal, interest rate, and minimum payments.\n- **Assets**: Tracks assets like bank accounts, investments, and property.\n- **Income**: Manages income sources, amounts, and frequency.\n- **Budgets**: Handles budget category management and monthly allocations.\n- **Sessions**: Secure session storage with `express-session` and PostgreSQL.\n- **Timber Analytics**: Daily aggregated statistics for Timber AI usage (interactions, messages, documents analyzed)\n\n### Subscription & Payment Flow\n1. **Authentication Required**: Users must log in with Replit Auth before subscribing to paid plans\n   - Pricing page waits for auth to finish loading before allowing plan selection\n   - Prevents race conditions with authentication state\n2. **Checkout Creation**: POST /api/checkout with plan parameter (requires authentication)\n   - Plans: pro_monthly, pro_annual, family_monthly, family_annual\n   - User ID and email automatically passed to Stripe as metadata\n3. **Payment Processing**: Users redirected to Stripe Checkout for secure payment\n4. **Session Verification**: After payment, users land on /subscription-success with session_id\n   - Page automatically calls GET /api/checkout/verify-session (public endpoint)\n   - Endpoint validates Stripe session and extracts userId from metadata (no auth required due to Stripe redirect)\n   - Security: Session validation through Stripe API, userId extracted from session metadata\n   - Handles pending payments (subscription=null or payment_status!='paid') with retry logic\n   - Updates user record only after confirming subscription exists and payment is complete\n5. **Subscription Tracking**: User subscription status stored in database for access control\n\n### UI/UX Decisions\n- Rebranded UI to emphasize the journey from financial chaos to automated management.\n- Implemented a bright emerald green primary color scheme.\n- Professional financial statements and clear legal pages enhance trust.\n- Mobile experience enhanced with photo capture for document upload.\n- Document upload error handling: try-finally blocks ensure UI always recovers from upload failures.\n\n### Document Processing Architecture\n1. **Dual Upload Systems**:\n   - **AI Vision Upload** (POST /api/documents/upload): GPT-4 vision analysis for intelligent extraction\n     - Status flow: processing → completed/failed\n     - Synchronous processing with error handling\n     - Auto-creates financial entities (debts, assets, income) from AI analysis\n   - **PDF/OCR Upload** (POST /api/docs/upload): Direct text extraction with enrichment pass\n     - PDF extraction using pdf-parse library\n     - Image OCR using Tesseract.js\n     - **S3 Storage**: Files uploaded to AWS S3 for cloud storage\n       - S3 key stored in database (s3Key field)\n       - Local files cleaned up after S3 upload\n       - Download endpoint (GET /api/docs/:id/download) generates signed URLs (1 hour expiry)\n       - Requires AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID, AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY, AWS_REGION, AWS_S3_BUCKET secrets\n     - **Enrichment Pass** (server/docs-parse-pass.ts):\n       - Period extraction: Captures statement date ranges (start/end)\n       - Transaction parsing: Extracts individual line items (up to 500 transactions)\n       - Validation: Compares transaction sum with stated total\n       - Adds period, transactions, and validations to analysisData\n     - Status flow: completed/failed (synchronous, no pending state)\n     - Includes needsReview flag for manual verification\n2. **Shoebox Mode** (Default): Users dump all documents at once\n   - Auto-detects document type from filename patterns\n   - AI categorizes and extracts data automatically\n   - Mobile-optimized with camera capture and photo library\n3. **Organized Mode**: Users select document type first, then upload\n   - Targeted upload for specific document categories\n   - More control over categorization\n4. **Error Handling**:\n   - AI analysis failures properly set document status to \"failed\"\n   - UI recovery guaranteed with try-finally blocks\n   - Per-file error handling prevents cascade failures\n   - Status filtering API: GET /api/docs?status={status}\n5. **Admin Dashboards**:\n   - **Timber Analytics** (/admin/timber): Real-time AI usage stats with daily analytics and charts\n   - **Documents Review** (/admin/docs): Document review interface with edit/approve capabilities\n     - Filter by status (completed, failed)\n     - View enriched analysisData with period, transactions, validations\n     - Edit fields: total, date, vendor, document type, notes\n     - Approve documents (marks as completed, removes review flag)\n   - Both protected by ADMIN_VIEW_KEY secret via x-admin-key header\n\n### Python Background Worker (worker.py)\n**Optional asynchronous document processing with advanced features:**\n\n1. **Worker Process**:\n   - Polls for documents with status: 'failed' (for retry processing)\n   - Respects MAX_RETRIES limit to prevent infinite retry loops\n   - Processes documents asynchronously in batches\n   - Uses database-level locking (FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED) to prevent conflicts\n   - Writes results to `documents.analysis_data`\n\n2. **Features**:\n   - **AWS Textract OCR** (✅ PRODUCTION-READY): Cloud-based OCR with **100% accuracy** on tested receipts\n     - Textract vs Tesseract comparison on sample receipt:\n       - **Textract**: Perfect extraction of \"Anytown, USA\", \"$12.34\", \"2025-10-15\" (100% accuracy)\n       - **Tesseract**: Errors like \"Anyiown\", \"$1234\" (missing decimals), date format issues (35% confidence)\n     - Configured via **AWS_TEXTRACT=1** environment variable\n     - Requires AWS credentials: AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID, AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY, AWS_REGION (stored in Replit Secrets)\n   - **Local OCR Fallback**: PDF and image processing using Tesseract and pdf2image (80-90% accuracy)\n   - **Improved Document Detection**: Enhanced logic detects receipts via \"thank you\", multiple price items, and \"total\" keyword\n   - **Decimal Point Correction**: Auto-corrects missing decimals (e.g., \"1234\" → \"12.34\")\n   - **Merchant Normalization**: Maps raw merchant names to standardized values\n   - **Smart Categorization**: Categorizes transactions (grocery, dining, travel, utilities, shopping)\n   - **Anomaly Detection**: Flags large amounts (>$5000) and negative totals\n   - **Confidence Scoring**: Assigns confidence scores to parsed data (75% for receipts with Textract)\n   - **Retry Logic**: Automatic retry with exponential backoff (max 3 attempts)\n\n3. **Document Parsing**:\n   - **Receipts**: Extracts merchant, date, total, category\n   - **Invoices**: Extracts invoice number, merchant, date, total, category\n   - **Statements**: Extracts transactions, period, totals\n\n4. **Configuration** (Environment Variables):\n   - `DATABASE_URL`: PostgreSQL connection string (auto-set by Replit)\n   - `AWS_S3_BUCKET`: S3 bucket name (required if using S3)\n   - `AWS_REGION`: AWS region (default: us-east-1)\n   - `AWS_USE`: Set to \"1\" to enable S3 downloads\n   - `AWS_TEXTRACT`: Set to \"1\" to enable AWS Textract OCR (superior accuracy, requires AWS credentials)\n   - `POLL_INTERVAL`: Seconds between polls (default: 6)\n   - `BATCH_SIZE`: Documents per batch (default: 5)\n   - `MAX_RETRIES`: Max retry attempts (default: 3)\n   - `ANOMALY_AMOUNT_THRESHOLD`: Dollar threshold for anomaly detection (default: 5000.0)\n   - `OCR_FALLBACK_API`: Optional external OCR API endpoint\n\n5. **Status Flow**:\n   - Documents with status 'failed' (and attempts < MAX_RETRIES) → processed\n   - Success → status 'completed', needs_review flag set based on confidence/anomalies\n   - High confidence, no anomalies → needs_review=false\n   - Low confidence or anomalies detected → needs_review=true\n   - Max retries exceeded → remains 'failed' with needs_review=true\n\n6. **Error Tracking**:\n   - `attempts`: Number of processing attempts\n   - `lastError`: Last error message (2000 char limit)\n   - `lastErrorAt`: Timestamp of last error\n\n7. **Running the Worker**:\n   ```bash\n   python3 worker.py\n   ```\n   Or create a workflow in Replit to run it automatically\n\n8. **Dependencies**:\n   - Python packages: sqlalchemy, psycopg2-binary, boto3, pytesseract, pillow, pdf2image, requests\n   - System packages: tesseract, poppler_utils\n\n## External Dependencies\n\n- **Replit Auth**: For secure user authentication (Google, GitHub, email/password).\n- **Stripe**: For subscription-based monetization, handling payments };\n\n  const processFiles = (newFiles: File[]) => {\n    const uploadedFiles: UploadedFile[] = newFiles.map((file) => ({\n      id: Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9),\n      name: file.name,\n if (progress >= 100) {\n        progress = 100;\n        clearInterval(interval);\n      }\n      setFiles((prev) =>\n        prev.map((f) => (f.id === fileId ? { ...f, progress } : f))\n      );\n className=\"space-y-6\">\n      <div\n        onDragOver={handleDragOver}\n        onDragLeave={handleDragLeave}\n        onDrop={handleDrop}\n        className={`\n          relative border-2    }\n        `}\n        data-testid=\"upload-zone\"\n      >\n        <input\n          type=\"file\"\n          multiple\n          accept=\".pdf,.png,.jpg,.jpeg,.csv\"\n \"@tanstack/react-query\";\nimport { useEffect } from \"react\";\n\nexport default function SubscriptionSuccess() {\n  const [, setLocation] = useLocation();\n  const searchParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);\n  const sessionId = searchParams.get('session_id');\n\n  // Verify the session and update user subscription\n  const { data, isLoading, error } = useQuery({\n    queryKey: ['/api/checkout/verify-session', sessionId],\n    queryFn: async () => {\n      if (!sessionId) {\n        throw new Error('No session ID found');\n      }\n      const response = await fetch(`/api/checkout/verify-session?session_id=${sessionId}`);\n      if (!response.ok) {\n        throw new Error('Failed to verify subscription');\n      }\n      return response.json();\n    },\n    enabled: !!sessionId,\n    retry: 3,\n    retryDelay: 2000, // Wait 2 seconds between retries for pending payments\n  });\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!sessionId) {\n      // Redirect to pricing if no session ID\n      setTimeout(() => setLocation('/pricing'), 2000);\n    }\n  }, [sessionId, setLocation]);\n\n  if (!sessionId) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"container max-w-2xl mx-auto py-16\">\n        <Card className=\"text-center\">\n          <CardContent className=\"py-8\">\n            <AlertCircle className=\"h-12 w-12 text-yellow-500 mx-auto mb-4\" />\n            <p className=\"text-muted-foreground\">No subscription session found. Stripe's clean, professional aesthetic combined with Material Design's robust component library provides the perfect foundation for a fintech tool requiring precision and user confidence.\n\n**Key Design Principles**:\n- Trust through minimalism and clarity\n- Data hierarchy that guides action\n- Professional, secure aesthetic\n- Action-oriented design for debt reduction\n\n## Core Design Elements\n\n### A. Color Palette\n\n**Dark Mode Primary** (default):\n- Background: 222 14% 8% (deep slate)\n- Surface: 222 14% 12% (elevated slate)\n- Surface Elevated: 222 14% 16%\n- Primary: 210 100% 60% (trust blue)\n- Success/Positive: 142 76% 45% (financial green)\n- Warning/Debt: 0 84% 60% (alert red)\n- Text Primary: 210 20% 98%\n- Text Secondary: 215 20% 65%\n- Border: 217 19% 27%\n\n**Light Mode**:\n- Background: 0 0% 100%\n- Surface: 210 20% 98%\n- Primary: 210 100% 50%\n- Success: 142 76% 36%\n- Warning: 0 72% 51%\n\n### B. Typography\n- **Primary Font**: Inter (Google Fonts) - clean, professional\n- **Monospace**: JetBrains Mono (for financial figures)\n- **Hierarchy**:\n  - H1: 2.5rem/3rem, font-bold (page titles)\n  - H2: 1.875rem/2.25rem, font-semibold (section headers)\n  - H3: 1.5rem/2rem, font-semibold (card titles)\n  - Body: 1rem/1.5rem, font-normal\n  - Financial Data: 1.125rem/1.75rem, font-mono, font-medium\n  - Small/Meta: 0.875rem/1.25rem, font-normal\n\n### C. Layout System\n**Spacing Primitives**: Tailwind units of 3, 4, 6, 8, 12, 16\n- Component padding: p-6 (cards), p-4 (compact elements)\n- Section spacing: space-y-8 (main layout), space-y-4 (grouped elements)\n- Grid gaps: gap-6 (dashboard cards), gap-4 (form fields)\n- Container max-width: max-w-7xl for main content\n\n### D. Component Library\n\n**Dashboard Cards**:\n- Rounded corners: rounded-xl\n- Shadow: shadow-lg with subtle border\n- Padding: p-6\n- Headers with icons and action buttons\n- Metric displays with large monospace numbers\n\n**Data Visualization**:\n- Charts using Chart.js/Recharts in brand colors\n- Debt reduction timeline with progress indicators\n- Spending breakdown pie/donut charts\n- Monthly comparison bar charts\n\n**Document Upload Zone**:\n- Dashed border drag-and-drop area\n- File type icons (PDF, CSV, bank statements)\n- Upload progress with percentage\n- Document preview thumbnails in grid\n\n**Financial Summary Components**:\n- Total debt counter (large, prominent)\n- Monthly payment calculator\n- Debt-free date projection\n- Savings potential indicator\n\n**Action Buttons**:\n- Primary: bg-primary with hover lift effect\n- Destructive: bg-warning for debt actions\n- Success: bg-success for completed actions\n- Sizes: Default (h-10), Large (h-12 for CTAs)\n\n**Navigation**:\n- Sidebar navigation (fixed left, w-64)\n- Icons with labels: Upload, Dashboard, Analysis, Budget Plan, Reports\n- Active state: bg-surface-elevated with left border accent\n- Collapsed mobile state with hamburger\n\n**Forms & Inputs**:\n- Input fields: h-10, rounded-lg, border focus:ring-2\n- Dark mode: bg-surface with border-border\n- Labels: text-sm font-medium mb-2\n- Validation states with colored borders\n\n**Tables**:\n- Striped rows for readability\n- Sticky headers for long lists\n- Sortable columns with arrow indicators\n- Action column (right-aligned) for row actions\n- Mobile: Card-based responsive layout\n\n**Data Overlays**:\n- Modal dialogs: centered, max-w-2xl, backdrop blur\n- Toast notifications: top-right, auto-dismiss\n- Confirmation dialogs for debt plan actions\n\n### E. Animations\n**Minimal, purposeful animations only**:\n- Button hover: subtle scale (scale-105) and shadow increase\n- Card hover: gentle lift (translate-y-[-2px])\n- Loading states: Skeleton screens (not spinners) for data\n- Chart animations: Smooth bar/line growth on load (300ms ease)\n- Page transitions: None (instant for data app)\n\n## Application-Specific Design\n\n**Dashboard Layout**:\n- 3-column grid on desktop (debt overview, recent uploads, action items)\n- 2-column on tablet, single-column mobile\n- Sticky metrics header showing total debt and progress\n\n**Upload Interface**:\n- Central drag-drop zone (80vh max-height)\n- Supported file icons and descriptions\n- AI processing indicator with step-by-step progress\n- Document classification preview\n\n**Analysis View**:\n- Split view: Document preview (left 40%) | AI insights (right 60%)\n- Highlighted transactions with categorization\n- Inline editing for corrections\n- Export analyzed data button\n\n**Budget Plan Display**:\n- Timeline visualization of debt payoff\n- Monthly breakdown accordion\n- \"Snowball vs Avalanche\" comparison toggle\n- Editable payment amounts with real-time recalculation\n\n**Trust Elements**:\n- \"Bank-level encryption\" badge in upload area\n- \"Your data is never stored\" messaging\n- Secure connection indicator in header\n- Clear data deletion options\n\nThis creates a professional, data-focused financial application that inspires trust while providing powerful debt management tools.","size_bytes":5125},"client/src/components/ui/input-otp.tsx":{"content":"import * upsertUser(userData: UpsertUser): Promise<User> {\n    const [user] = await db\n      .insert(users)\n      .values(userData)\n      .onConflictDoUpdate({\n        target: users.id,\n        set: {\n          ...userData,\n          updatedAt: new Date(),\n        },\n      })\n      .returning();\n    return user;\n  }\n\n  async updateUserStripeInfo(\n    userId: string,\n    customerId: .from(documents)\n      .where(eq(documents.userId, userId))\n      .orderBy(desc(documents.uploadedAt));\n  }\n\n  async updateDocumentStatus(\n    id: string,\n    status: string,\n    analysisData?: any\n  ): Promise<Document> {\n    const [document] = await db\n      .update(documents)\n      .set({\n        status,\n        processedAt: status === \"completed\" ? new Date() : .set({ ...updates, updatedAt: new Date() })\n      .where(eq(debts.id, id))\n      .returning();\n    return debt;\n  }\n\n  async deleteDebt(id: string): Promise<void> {\n    await from \"react\"\n\nimport type {\n  ToastActionElement,\n  ToastProps,\n} from \"@/components/ui/toast\"\n\nconst TOAST_LIMIT = 1\nconst TOAST_REMOVE_DELAY = 1000000\n\ntype ToasterToast = ToastProps & {\n  id: string\n  title?: React.ReactNode\n  description?: React.ReactNode\n  action?: ToastActionElement\n}\n\nconst actionTypes = {\n  ADD_TOAST: \"ADD_TOAST\",\n  UPDATE_TOAST: \"UPDATE_TOAST\",\n  DISMISS_TOAST: \"DISMISS_TOAST\",\n  REMOVE_TOAST: \"REMOVE_TOAST\",\n} as const\n\nlet count = 0\n\nfunction genId() {\n  count = (count + 1) % Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER\n  return count.toString()\n}\n\ntype ActionType = typeof actionTypes\n\ntype Action =\n  | {\n      type: ActionType[\"ADD_TOAST\"]\n      toast: ToasterToast\n    }\n  | {\n      type: ActionType[\"UPDATE_TOAST\"]\n      toast: Partial<ToasterToast>\n    }\n  | {\n      type: ActionType[\"DISMISS_TOAST\"]\n      toastId?: ToasterToast[\"id\"]\n    }\n  | {\n      type: ActionType[\"REMOVE_TOAST\"]\n      toastId?: ToasterToast[\"id\"]\n    }\n\ninterface State {\n  toasts: ToasterToast[]\n}\n\nconst toastTimeouts = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>()\n\nconst addToRemoveQueue = (toastId: string) => {\n  if (toastTimeouts.has(toastId)) {\n    return\n  }\n\n  const timeout = setTimeout(() => {\n    toastTimeouts.delete(toastId)\n    dispatch({\n      type: \"REMOVE_TOAST\",\n      toastId: toastId,\n    })\n  }, TOAST_REMOVE_DELAY)\n\n  toastTimeouts.set(toastId, timeout)\n}\n\nexport const reducer = (state: State, action: Action): State => {\n  switch (action.type) {\n    case \"ADD_TOAST\":\n      return {\n        ...state,\n        toasts: [action.toast, ...state.toasts].slice(0, TOAST_LIMIT),\n      }\n\n    case \"UPDATE_TOAST\":\n      return {\n        ...state,\n        toasts: state.toasts.map((t) =>\n          t.id === action.toast.id ? { ...t, ...action.toast } : t\n        ),\n      }\n\n    case \"DISMISS_TOAST\": {\n      const { toastId } = action\n\n      // ! Side effects ! - This could be extracted into a dismissToast() action,\n      // but I'll keep it here for simplicity\n      if (toastId) {\n        addToRemoveQueue(toastId)\n      } else {\n        state.toasts.forEach((toast) => {\n          addToRemoveQueue(toast.id)\n        })\n      }\n\n      return {\n        ...state,\n        toasts: state.toasts.map((t) =>\n          t.id === toastId || toastId === undefined\n            ? {\n                ...t,\n                open: false,\n              }\n            : t\n        ),\n      }\n    }\n    case \"REMOVE_TOAST\":\n      if !== action.toastId),\n      }\n  }\n}\n\nconst listeners: Array<(state: State) => void> = []\n\nlet memoryState: State = { toasts: [] }\n\nfunction dispatch(action: Action) {\n  memoryState = reducer(memoryState, action)\n  listeners.forEach((listener) => {\n    listener(memoryState)\n  })\n}\n\ntype Toast = Omit<ToasterToast, \"id\">\n\nfunction toast({ ...props }: Toast) {\n  const id = genId()\n\n  const update = (props: ToasterToast) =>\n    dispatch({\n      type: \"UPDATE_TOAST\",\n      toast: { ...props, id },\n    })\n  const dismiss = () => dispatch({ type: \"DISMISS_TOAST\", toastId: id })\n\n  dispatch({\n    type: \"ADD_TOAST\",\n    toast: {\n      ...props,\n      id,\n      open: true,\n      onOpenChange: (open) => {\n        if (!open) dismiss()\n      },\n    },\n  })\n\n  return {\n    id: id,\n    dismiss,\n    update,\n  }\n}\n\nfunction useToast() {\n  const [state, setState] = React.useState<State>(memoryState)\n\n  React.useEffect(() => {\n    listeners.push(setState)\n    return () => {\n      const index = listeners.indexOf(setState)\n      if (index > -1) {\n        listeners.splice(index, 1)\n      }\n    }\n  }, [state])\n\n  return Please wait a moment.\",\n          paymentStatus: session.payment_status,\n        });\n      }\n\n      // Extract subscription details (safe now that we've checked it exists)\n      const subscription = session.subscription as Stripe.Subscription;\n      const plan = session.metadata?.plan || 'unknown';\n\n      // Import storage to update user\n      const { storage } = await import(\"./storage\");\n\n      // Update user with subscription info\n      await storage.updateUserSubscription(userId, {\n        stripeCustomerId: session.customer as string,\n        stripeSubscriptionId: subscription.id,\n        subscriptionStatus: subscription.status,\n        subscriptionPlan: plan,\n      });\n\n      return res.json({\n        success: true,\n        subscription: {\n          id: subscription.id,\n          status: subscription.status,\n          plan: plan,\n          currentPeriodEnd: (subscription as any).current_period_end || null,\n        },\n      });\n    } catch (err: any) {\n      console.error(\"Session verification error:\", err);\n      return res.status(500).json({ message: err.message || \"Verification error\" });\n    }\n  });\n}\n","size_bytes":5159},"STRIPE_SETUP.md":{"content":"# Stripe Product Setup Guide\n\n## Required Stripe Products & Prices\n\nYou need to create the following products and prices in your Stripe Dashboard:\n\n### 1. date: string|null; vendor: string|null };\n  period?: { start?: string|null; end?: string|null };\n  transactions?: { date?: string; desc?: string; amount?: number }[];\n  validations?: { totalMatches?: boolean; notes?: string[] };\n  preview?: string[];\n};\n\nexport function enrich(text: string, base: Enriched): Enriched {\n  const out: Enriched = { ...base, validations: { notes: [] } };\n\n  // Statement period\n  const period = text.match(/\\b(period|from)\\s*[:-]?\\s*(\\d{1,2}[\\/.\\-]\\d{1,2}[\\/.\\-]20\\d{2})\\s*(to|-|through)\\s*(\\d{1,2}[\\/.\\-]\\d{1,2}[\\/.\\-]20\\d{2})/i);\n  if (period) out.period = { start: period[2], end: period[4] };\n\n  // Transactions (very lenient)\n  const tx: { date?: string; desc?: string; amount?: number }[] = [];\n  const lines = text.split(/\\r?\\n/).map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 2000);\n  const txRe = /^(\\d{1,2}[\\/\\-]\\d{1,2})(?:[\\/\\-](\\d{2,4}))?\\s+(.+?)\\s+(-?\\$?\\d{1,3}(?:,\\d{3})*(?:\\.\\d{2})?)$/;\n  for (const line of lines) {\n    const m = line.match(txRe);\n    if (m) {\n      const date = m[1] + (m[2] ? '/' + m[2] : '');\n      const desc = m[3].replace(/\\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 120);\n      const amt = Number(String(m[4]).replace(/[^0-9.-]/g, ''));\n      if (!Number.isNaN(amt)) tx.push({ date, desc, amount: amt });\n    }\n  }\n  if (tx.length) out.transactions = tx.slice(0, 500);\n\n  // Validate total (rough: sum of positive lines equals stated total)\n  const stated = out.summary?.total ? Number(String(out.summary.total).replace(/[^0-9.-]/g,'')) : null;\n  if (stated != null && tx.length) {\n    const sum = tx.filter(t=>t.amount&&t.amount>0).reduce((a,b)=>a+(b.amount||0),0);\n    out.validations = out.validations || {};\n    out.validations.totalMatches = Math.abs(sum - stated) < 0.01;\n    if (!out.validations.totalMatches) out.validations.notes = [...(out.validations.notes||[]), `Sum ${sum} != stated ${stated}`];\n  }\n\n  return out;\n}\n","size_bytes":1948},"client/src/pages/PitchAccessAdmin.tsx":{"content":"import { useState } from
